```python
def test_fingerprint_covers_package_sources():
    """Fingerprint differs from a pyproject-only digest -> package is hashed."""
    import hashlib

    repo_pyproject = SRC.parent / "pyproject.toml"
    only = hashlib.sha256()
    only.update(repo_pyproject.read_bytes())
    assert _source_fingerprint() != only.hexdigest()
```

```python
def test_package_file_change_changes_fingerprint():
    probe = SRC / "package" / "_fingerprint_probe_tmp.py"
    assert not probe.exists()
    try:
        before = _source_fingerprint()
        probe.write_text("X = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
        assert _source_fingerprint() != before
    finally:
        probe.unlink(missing_ok=True)
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_unrelated_file_does_not_change_fingerprint():
    # experiments/ is outside package/scripts/configs and not .py/.yaml there
    probe = SRC / "experiments" / "_fingerprint_probe_tmp.py"
    probe.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    try:
        before = _source_fingerprint()
        probe.write_text("X = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
        assert _source_fingerprint() == before
    finally:
        probe.unlink(missing_ok=True)
```

```python
def test_step_count_match_catches_phantom_step(evaluator):
    """exec_018 shape: empty plan must produce zero steps — a phantom step
    keeps every legacy metric at 1.0 but must fail step_count_match."""
    exp = {"all_steps_ok": True, "step_error_kinds": [], "bad_reference_count": 0,
           "num_steps": 0, "step_outputs": []}
    phantom = {"steps": [{"tool": "FilterTool", "ok": True, "args": {},
                          "data": {"candidates": []}, "error_kind": None}]}
    m = evaluator.score_component(phantom, exp)
    assert m["step_count_match"] == 0.0
    assert m["all_steps_ok"] == 1.0  # legacy metric alone would NOT catch it
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_real_tool_passes_all_cases(cases, evaluator):
    """Every correctness metric must be 1.0 (step_success_rate is a raw
    rate and legitimately <1.0 on expected-failure cases)."""
    bad = []
    for c in cases:
        m = evaluator.score_component(evaluator.run_component(c.input), c.expected)
        fails = {k: m[k] for k in CORRECTNESS_METRICS if k in m and m[k] != 1.0}
        if fails:
            bad.append((c.case_id, fails))
    assert bad == [], f"cases not at 1.0: {bad}"
```

```python
def test_grounding_benchmark_with_fake_jev():
    """Test grounding benchmark end-to-end with mocked Jev provider."""
    # Set fake API key for config expansion
    os.environ["TYPESAFE_API_KEY"] = "fake_test_key"

    with patch("httpx.Client") as mock_client_class:
        mock_client = Mock()
        mock_client_class.return_value = mock_client

        # Mock Jev response
        mock_response = Mock()
        mock_response.status_code = 200
        mock_response.json.return_value = {
            "answers": {
                "claim_0": {"noul": 0.9, "confidence": 0.95},
                "claim_1": {"noul": 0.8, "confidence": 0.9},
            },
            "usage": {"input_tokens": 10, "output_tokens": 5},
        }
        mock_client.post.return_value = mock_response

        benchmark = GroundingBenchmark(config={"use_jev": True})

        case_input = {
            "case_id": "grounding_001",
            "input": {
                "user_request": "I want an action game",
                "evidence": [
                    {"item_id": "vg_001", "title": "Action Game", "main_category": "Action", "price": 30.0}
                ],
            },
            "expected": {
                "claims_supported": 1,
                "total_claims": 1,
                "grounding_rate": 1.0,
                "hallucination_rate": 0.0,
            },
        }

        actual_output = benchmark.run_component(case_input["input"])
        metrics = benchmark.score_component(actual_output, case_input["expected"])

        # Verify Jev was called and produced results
        assert "jev_result" in actual_output
        assert actual_output["jev_result"]["evaluation_state"] == "success"
        assert actual_output["jev_result"]["total_claims"] > 0  # Realistic answer produces claims

        # Verify metrics are computed
        assert metrics["total_claims"] > 0
        assert 0.0 <= metrics["grounding_rate"] <= 1.0
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/component/benchmarks/test_grounding_phase1.py::test_partial_judge_error_counted_separately`  (0.0023s)

_Faithfulness batch fails but communication succeeds -> judge_error metric, run ok._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_partial_judge_error_counted_separately(monkeypatch):
    """Faithfulness batch fails but communication succeeds -> judge_error metric, run ok."""
    _patched_judge_config(monkeypatch)
    monkeypatch.delenv("TYPESAFE_API_KEY", raising=False)

    with patch("httpx.Client") as cls:
        client = Mock()
        cls.return_value = client
        client.post.side_effect = [
            Exception("jev down"),
            _resp({"relevance": {"score": 3}}),
        ]
        bench = GroundingBenchmark(config=_config())
        out = bench.run_component(_case_input())

    assert out["evidence_kind"] == "real_inference_output"  # Phase 4.3: real compose_answer output
    jev_result = out["jev_result"]
    assert jev_result["evaluation_state"] == "partial_error"
    assert jev_result["judge_error_count"] == 1

    metrics = bench.score_component(out, {})
    assert metrics["judge_error_count"] == 1
    # unsupported_count must equal the number of verdict=="unsupported" claims —
    # judge_error claims are excluded from both supported and unsupported counts.
    n_unsupported = sum(1 for c in jev_result["claims"] if c["verdict"] == "unsupported")
    assert metrics["unsupported_count"] == n_unsupported
    assert metrics["hallucination_rate"] == pytest.approx(
        n_unsupported / max(n_unsupported + jev_result["supported_count"], 1) if (n_unsupported + jev_result["supported_count"]) else 0.0
    )
```

```python
def test_label_agreement_partial_and_zero():
    """A wrong expected label must be caught: agreement < 1, and 0 when all wrong."""
    bench = GroundingBenchmark(config=_config())
    # Jev says supported; expected disagrees on 2 of the 3 asserted keys
    expected = {"claims_supported": 1, "grounding_rate": 1.0, "hallucination_rate": 1.0}
    metrics = bench.score_component(_actual_output(), expected)
    assert metrics["label_agreement"] == pytest.approx(2 / 3)
    all_wrong = {"claims_supported": 0, "grounding_rate": 0.0, "hallucination_rate": 1.0}
    assert bench.score_component(_actual_output(), all_wrong)["label_agreement"] == 0.0
```

```python
def test_aggregate_handles_missing_grounded_rate():
    """Template-mode results carry no grounded_rate; aggregation must not
    crash and must only average keys actually present."""
    results = [
        _result({"contract_valid": 1.0, "no_mismatch": 1.0}),
        _result({"contract_valid": 0.0, "no_mismatch": 1.0}),
        _result({"contract_valid": 1.0, "no_mismatch": 0.0,
                 "grounded_rate": 1.0}),
    ]
    agg = ComponentEvaluator._aggregate_metrics(None, results)
    assert agg["contract_valid_mean"] == pytest.approx(2 / 3)
    assert agg["grounded_rate_mean"] == 1.0  # mean over the one case that has it
    assert "grounded_rate_std" in agg
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/component/benchmarks/test_integration_dataset.py::test_presented_ids_exact_detects_drop_and_reorder`  (0.0016s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_presented_ids_exact_detects_drop_and_reorder(evaluator):
    row = {"item_id": VG + "vg_a", "price": 1.0}
    exp = {"presented_item_ids": [VG + "vg_a", VG + "vg_b"]}
    produced_items = {
        VG + "vg_a": row,
        VG + "vg_b": {"item_id": VG + "vg_b", "price": 150.0},
    }

    def actual(ids):
        return {
            "steps": [],
            "answer": {"status": "ok",
                       "items": [dict(produced_items[i]) for i in ids],
                       "grounded": True},
            "produced_item_ids": list(produced_items),
            "produced_items": produced_items,
        }

    assert evaluator.score_component(
        actual([VG + "vg_a", VG + "vg_b"]), exp)["presented_ids_exact"] == 1.0
    # dropped item
    assert evaluator.score_component(
        actual([VG + "vg_a"]), exp)["presented_ids_exact"] == 0.0
    # right ids, wrong order
    assert evaluator.score_component(
        actual([VG + "vg_b", VG + "vg_a"]), exp)["presented_ids_exact"] == 0.0
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/component/benchmarks/test_integration_dataset.py::test_item_fields_match_detects_field_mutation`  (0.0015s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_item_fields_match_detects_field_mutation(evaluator):
    produced_items = {
        VG + "vg_a": {"item_id": VG + "vg_a", "price": 1.0, "title": "T"},
    }
    base = {
        "steps": [],
        "produced_item_ids": [VG + "vg_a"],
        "produced_items": produced_items,
    }
    good = dict(base, answer={"status": "ok", "items": [
        {"item_id": VG + "vg_a", "price": 1.0, "title": "T"}], "grounded": True})
    mutated = dict(base, answer={"status": "ok", "items": [
        {"item_id": VG + "vg_a", "price": 2.0, "title": "T"}], "grounded": True})
    dropped = dict(base, answer={"status": "ok", "items": [
        {"item_id": VG + "vg_a"}], "grounded": True})

    assert evaluator.score_component(good, {})["item_fields_match"] == 1.0
    assert evaluator.score_component(mutated, {})["item_fields_match"] == 0.0
    # dropped optional field is allowed only if produced row lacks it too —
    # here produced has price/title, so omitting them must NOT pass silently:
    # comparison covers fields present in either side.
    assert evaluator.score_component(dropped, {})["item_fields_match"] == 0.0
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/component/benchmarks/test_integration_dataset.py::test_real_tools_pass_all_cases`  (0.0287s)

_Every case must satisfy contract_valid/no_mismatch plus the asserted_

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_real_tools_pass_all_cases(cases, evaluator):
    """Every case must satisfy contract_valid/no_mismatch plus the asserted
    metrics (presented_ids_exact, item_fields_match where present)."""
    bad = []
    for c in cases:
        m = evaluator.score_component(evaluator.run_component(c.input), c.expected)
        fails = {k: m[k] for k in ("contract_valid", "no_mismatch",
                                   "presented_ids_exact", "item_fields_match")
                 if k in m and m[k] != 1.0}
        if fails:
            bad.append((c.case_id, fails))
    assert bad == [], f"cases not at 1.0: {bad}"
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/component/benchmarks/test_memory_oracle.py::test_oracle_basic_love_hate`  (0.0242s)

_Test oracle computes love/hate correctly with mixed ratings._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_oracle_basic_love_hate(meta_df):
    """Test oracle computes love/hate correctly with mixed ratings."""
    interactions = pd.DataFrame({
        "user_id": ["u_mix"] * 5,
        "item_id": ["Video_Games::vg_a", "Video_Games::vg_b", "Video_Games::vg_c",
                   "Video_Games::vg_d", "Video_Games::vg_e"],
        "user_idx": [0] * 5,
        "item_idx": [0, 1, 2, 3, 4],
        "rating": [5, 4, 3, 2, 1],
        "timestamp": [0, 1, 2, 3, 4],
    })
    user2id = {"u_mix": 0}
    item2id = {"Video_Games::vg_a": 0, "Video_Games::vg_b": 1, "Video_Games::vg_c": 2,
               "Video_Games::vg_d": 3, "Video_Games::vg_e": 4}

    oracle_result = compute_memory_oracle(
        interactions, "u_mix", user2id, item2id, meta_df,
        top_n_categories=5, recent_n=10,
        love_min_rating=4, hate_max_rating=2,
        top_n_loved_items=10, top_n_hated_items=10
    )

    # Love: ratings 5 and 4 (vg_a, vg_b)
    assert "Video_Games::vg_a" in oracle_result["loved_items"]
    assert "Video_Games::vg_b" in oracle_result["loved_items"]
    assert "Video_Games::vg_c" not in oracle_result["loved_items"]  # rating 3

    # Hate: ratings 2 and 1 (vg_d, vg_e)
    assert "Video_Games::vg_d" in oracle_result["hated_items"]
    assert "Video_Games::vg_e" in oracle_result["hated_items"]
    assert "Video_Games::vg_c" not in oracle_result["hated_items"]  # rating 3
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/component/benchmarks/test_memory_oracle.py::test_oracle_with_as_of_timestamp`  (0.0143s)

_Test oracle respects as_of_timestamp filtering._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_oracle_with_as_of_timestamp(meta_df):
    """Test oracle respects as_of_timestamp filtering."""
    early_interactions = pd.DataFrame({
        "user_id": ["u_snapshot"] * 5,
        "item_id": ["Video_Games::vg_a", "Video_Games::vg_b", "Video_Games::vg_c",
                   "Video_Games::vg_d", "Video_Games::vg_e"],
        "user_idx": [0] * 5,
        "item_idx": [0, 1, 2, 3, 4],
        "rating": [5] * 5,
        "timestamp": [0, 1, 2, 3, 4],
    })

    late_interactions = pd.DataFrame({
        "user_id": ["u_snapshot"] * 5,
        "item_id": ["Video_Games::vg_a", "Video_Games::vg_b", "Video_Games::vg_c",
                   "Video_Games::vg_d", "Video_Games::vg_e"],
        "user_idx": [0] * 5,
        "item_idx": [0, 1, 2, 3, 4],
        "rating": [1] * 5,
        "timestamp": [5, 6, 7, 8, 9],
    })

    interactions = pd.concat([early_interactions, late_interactions])
    user2id = {"u_snapshot": 0}
    item2id = {"Video_Games::vg_a": 0, "Video_Games::vg_b": 1, "Video_Games::vg_c": 2,
               "Video_Games::vg_d": 3, "Video_Games::vg_e": 4}

    oracle_result = compute_memory_oracle(
        interactions, "u_snapshot", user2id, item2id, meta_df,
        top_n_categories=5, recent_n=10, as_of_timestamp=4,
        love_min_rating=4, hate_max_rating=2,
        top_n_loved_items=10, top_n_hated_items=10
    )

    # Should only see interactions up to timestamp 4 (rating 5)
    assert oracle_result["n_interactions"] == 5
    assert "Video_Games::vg_a" in oracle_result["loved_items"]
    assert "Video_Games::vg_a" not in oracle_result["hated_items"]
```

```python
def test_requires_clarification_consistent(cases):
```

```python
def test_expected_steps_pass_schema_validation(cases, executor):
    tools_by_name = dict(executor.tools)  # PlanExecutor stores tools as {name: Tool}
    offenders = []
    for c in cases:
        for i, step in enumerate(c.expected.get("steps", []), 1):
            err = _step_error(step, i, tools_by_name)
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/component/benchmarks/test_planner_dataset.py::test_expected_plans_execute_clean`  (0.0528s)

_Run each expected plan on the real tools: every step ok, last result non-empty._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_expected_plans_execute_clean(cases, executor):
    """Run each expected plan on the real tools: every step ok, last result non-empty."""
    failures = []
    for c in cases:
        steps = c.expected.get("steps", [])
        if not steps:
            continue  # ask_user cases produce no plan to execute
        results = executor._execute(steps, c.input.get("user_request", ""))
        for r in results:
            if not r.get("ok"):
                failures.append(f"{c.case_id}: {r.get('tool')} failed: {r.get('error')} (kind={r.get('error_kind')})")
        last = results[-1] if results else {}
        if results and not _has_content(last.get("data") or {}):
            failures.append(f"{c.case_id}: final step returned empty data")
    assert failures == [], "expected plans do not execute on fixture tools:\n" + "\n".join(failures)
```

```python
def test_plan_validity_mutation_unknown_tool_arg_ref(executor):
    """A plan with a fake tool, an undeclared arg, and a forward ref must score 0."""
    bench = _bench(_tools_by_name(executor))
    bad_plan = [
        {"tool": "FakeTool", "args": {}},
        {"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"filters": {"categories_none": ["RPG"]}}},
        {"tool": "LookupTool", "args": {"item_ids": "$9.candidates.*.item_id"}},
    ]
    actual = {"steps": bad_plan, "question": None}
    expected = {"steps": [], "question": None}
    m = bench.score_component(actual, expected)
    assert m["plan_validity"] == 0.0
    assert m["step_validity_rate"] == 0.0
```

```python
def test_plan_validity_partial(executor):
    """One bad step out of three -> step_validity_rate = 2/3, plan_validity = 0."""
    bench = _bench(_tools_by_name(executor))
    steps = [
        {"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"filters": {"domain": "Video_Games"}, "limit": 5}},
        {"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {"sort_by": "price"}},
        {"tool": "LookupTool", "args": {"item_ids": ["Video_Games::vg_a"]}},
    ]
    m = bench.score_component({"steps": steps, "question": None}, {"steps": [], "question": None})
    assert m["step_validity_rate"] == pytest.approx(2 / 3)
    assert m["plan_validity"] == 0.0
```

```python
def test_clarification_precision_token_f1(executor):
    bench = _bench(_tools_by_name(executor))
    expected = {"steps": [], "question": "What type of games are you looking for?", "requires_clarification": True}
    # differently worded but same content -> high F1, not 0
    m = bench.score_component(
        {"steps": [], "question": "Which kind of games do you want?"}, expected
    )
    assert 0.0 < m["clarification_precision"] < 1.0
    assert m["clarification_recall"] == 1.0
    # exact match -> 1.0
    m2 = bench.score_component(
        {"steps": [], "question": "What type of games are you looking for?"}, expected
    )
    assert m2["clarification_precision"] == 1.0
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/component/benchmarks/test_planner_dataset.py::test_refusal_vs_unnecessary_clarification`  (0.0037s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_refusal_vs_unnecessary_clarification(executor):
    bench = _bench(_tools_by_name(executor))
    flagged = {"steps": [], "question": "q?", "requires_clarification": True}
    # guessed instead of asking -> refusal_to_guess = 0, key present
    m = bench.score_component({"steps": [{"tool": "FilterTool", "args": {}}], "question": None}, flagged)
    assert m["refusal_to_guess"] == 0.0
    assert "unnecessary_clarification_rate" not in m
    # asked properly -> 1.0
    m = bench.score_component({"steps": [], "question": "q?"}, flagged)
    assert m["refusal_to_guess"] == 1.0
    # unflagged case: asking anyway -> unnecessary_clarification_rate = 0, refusal absent
    m = bench.score_component({"steps": [], "question": "q?"}, {"steps": [], "question": None})
    assert m["unnecessary_clarification_rate"] == 0.0
    assert "refusal_to_guess" not in m
    # unflagged, no question -> 1.0
    m = bench.score_component({"steps": [], "question": None}, {"steps": [], "question": None})
    assert m["unnecessary_clarification_rate"] == 1.0
```

```python
def test_planner_unanswerable_cases_declare_clarification():
    cases = {c.case_id: c for c in load_cases(BENCH / "planner_dataset.jsonl")}
    for cid in ("planner_033", "planner_034"):
        c = cases[cid]
        assert c.expected["steps"] == []
        assert c.expected.get("requires_clarification") is True
        assert c.expected.get("question")
    # planner_035 is the boundary counterpart: answerable-but-empty -> run the
    # tool, do NOT clarify.
    c = cases["planner_035"]
    assert c.expected["steps"] and c.expected["steps"][0]["tool"] == "FilterTool"
    assert c.expected.get("question") is None
    assert "requires_clarification" not in c.expected
```

```python
def test_filter_cases_by_metadata():
    """Test filtering cases by metadata (AND logic)."""
    cases = [
        BenchmarkCase(case_id="c1", input={}, expected={}, metadata={"difficulty": "easy", "category": "memory"}),
        BenchmarkCase(case_id="c2", input={}, expected={}, metadata={"difficulty": "hard", "category": "memory"}),
        BenchmarkCase(case_id="c3", input={}, expected={}, metadata={"difficulty": "easy", "category": "planner"}),
    ]

    # Filter by single key
    filtered = filter_cases_by_metadata(cases, {"difficulty": "easy"})
    assert len(filtered) == 2
    assert {c.case_id for c in filtered} == {"c1", "c3"}

    # Filter by multiple keys (AND logic)
    filtered = filter_cases_by_metadata(cases, {"difficulty": "easy", "category": "memory"})
    assert len(filtered) == 1
    assert filtered[0].case_id == "c1"

    # No filter
    filtered = filter_cases_by_metadata(cases, {})
    assert len(filtered) == 3
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_oracle_matches_hand_computed_literals(world):
    """Expected values below are worked out by hand from the fixture, NOT produced by any code."""
    result = _oracle(world, "u_short", 2)
    assert result["user_id"] == "u_short"
    assert result["known"] is True
    assert result["n_interactions"] == 2
    assert result["top_categories"] == ["Action", "Puzzle"]
    assert result["price_range"] == {"min": 1.0, "max": 150.0}
    assert result["recent_items"] == ["Video_Games::vg_b", "Video_Games::vg_a"]
    # u_short has ratings 5 and 4, both >= love_min_rating=4
    assert "Video_Games::vg_a" in result["loved_items"]
    assert "Video_Games::vg_b" in result["loved_items"]
    assert result["hated_items"] == []

    assert _oracle(world, "u_outlier_price", 2)["price_range"] == {"min": 1.0, "max": 1000.0}
    assert _oracle(world, "u_equal_prices", 2)["price_range"] == {"min": 1.0, "max": 1.0}
    assert _oracle(world, "u_null_prices", 2)["price_range"] is None
    # u_dup_items: vg_b (Puzzle) twice + vg_a (Action) once -> distinct-item tie, alphabetical -> Action
    dup = _oracle(world, "u_dup_items", 1)
    assert dup["top_categories"] == ["Action"] and dup["n_interactions"] == 3
    # cutoff is inclusive: ts<=4 of u_leakage_test keeps items a..e only
    cut = _oracle(world, "u_leakage_test", 3, as_of=4)
    assert cut["n_interactions"] == 5
    assert cut["recent_items"][0] == "Video_Games::vg_e"
    assert _oracle(world, "u_leakage_test", 3, as_of=-1)["known"] is False
```

```python
def test_oracle_recent_n_matches_tool_config():
    # DEFAULT_RECENT_N is the oracle's default; MemoryTool now reads from memory.yaml
    # The value should match the config in memory.yaml (recent_items: 10)
    from package.agents._limits import get_memory_limits
    limits = get_memory_limits()
    assert DEFAULT_RECENT_N == limits.recent_items
```

```python
def test_oracle_does_not_depend_on_tool_or_corpus():
    import ast
    import package.evaluation.component.benchmarks.memory_oracle as mod
    tree = ast.parse(open(mod.__file__, encoding="utf-8").read())
    imported = {n.module or "" for n in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(n, ast.ImportFrom)} | {
        a.name for n in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(n, ast.Import) for a in n.names}
    assert not any(m.startswith(("package.memory", "package.tools")) for m in imported)
    assert not any(isinstance(n, ast.Attribute) and n.attr == "select" for n in ast.walk(tree))
```

```python
def test_committed_dataset_matches_oracle():
    # loved_items/hated_items order IS part of the contract ("highest/lowest-rated
    # items" — ranking order), and the oracle/tool sort is now deterministic
    # (rating, then item_id tie-break), so strict equality must hold.
    committed = builder.DATASET_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert committed == builder.render(), \
        "run: python -m package.evaluation.component.benchmarks.build_memory_correctness_dataset"
```

```python
def test_real_tool_passes_every_case(world):
    cases = load_cases(builder.DATASET_PATH)
    # Only test the original 20 cases (correctness_001 to correctness_020);
    # expansion cases (021-031, minus removed duplicates) are covered by
    # dedicated tests below.
    original_cases = [c for c in cases if c.case_id <= "correctness_020"]
    summary = _evaluator(world).run_benchmark(original_cases, component_name="memory_correctness")
    bad = [r.case_id for r in summary.per_case_results if r.metrics.get("all_fields_match") != 1.0]
    assert bad == []
    assert summary.metrics["future_leakage_mean"] == 0.0
    assert summary.metrics["success_rate"] == 1.0
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/component/test_memory_correctness.py::test_detector_catches_planted_leakage`  (0.0801s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_detector_catches_planted_leakage(world):
    inter, user2id, item2id, meta = world

    # Planted bug: the tool ignores the snapshot cut — it always sees the FULL
    # frame even though the evaluator hands it a pre-truncated frame for
    # "cutoff" cases. The leakage detector MUST flag the resulting profile.
    class FullFrame(MemoryTool):
        def __init__(self, interactions, user2id, item2id, corpus, logger=None, as_of_timestamp=None):
            super().__init__(inter, user2id, item2id, corpus, logger, as_of_timestamp=None)

    cases = [c for c in load_cases(builder.DATASET_PATH) if "cutoff" in c.tags]
    summary = _evaluator(world, tool_factory=FullFrame).run_benchmark(cases, component_name="leaky")
    assert summary.metrics["future_leakage_max"] == 1.0
    by_id = {r.case_id: r for r in summary.per_case_results}
    assert by_id["correctness_007"].metrics["future_leakage"] == 1.0
    assert by_id["correctness_007"].metrics["all_fields_match"] == 0.0
    assert by_id["correctness_008"].metrics["future_leakage"] == 0.0  # cutoff == last ts: nothing to leak
```

```python
def test_detector_flags_guard_that_stops_raising(world):
    class NoGuard(MemoryTool):
        def __init__(self, interactions, user2id, item2id, corpus, logger=None, as_of_timestamp=None):
            super().__init__(interactions, user2id, item2id, corpus, logger, as_of_timestamp=None)

    cases = [c for c in load_cases(builder.DATASET_PATH) if "leakage_guard" in c.tags and "negative" in c.tags]
    s = _evaluator(world, tool_factory=NoGuard).run_benchmark(cases, component_name="noguard")
    assert s.metrics["error_behavior_correct_mean"] == 0.0
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/component/test_memory_correctness.py::test_tool_side_asof_case_031_passes`  (0.0309s)

_correctness_031 isolates the execute()-level ts<=as_of filter:_

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_tool_side_asof_case_031_passes(world):
    """correctness_031 isolates the execute()-level ts<=as_of filter:
    untruncated frame + as_of assigned post-construction (guard bypassed).
    If that filter were removed, the profile would contain ts>4 rows."""
    cases = {c.case_id: c for c in load_cases(builder.DATASET_PATH)}
    case = cases["correctness_031"]
    assert case.input["snapshot_mode"] == "tool_side_asof"
    assert case.metadata["production_path"] is False
    ev = _evaluator(world)
    out = ev.run_component(case.input)
    assert "error_type" not in out
    metrics = ev.score_component(out, case.expected)
    assert metrics["all_fields_match"] == 1.0
    assert metrics["n_interactions_correct"] == 1.0
    assert metrics.get("future_leakage", 0.0) == 0.0
```

_Mutation check: a tool whose execute() ignores as_of_timestamp must_

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_tool_side_asof_catches_removed_execute_filter(world):
    """Mutation check: a tool whose execute() ignores as_of_timestamp must
    fail case 031 — proving the case exercises the tool-side filter."""
    class NoExecuteFilter(MemoryTool):
        def execute(self, user_id, top_n_categories=None):
            saved = self.as_of_timestamp
            self.as_of_timestamp = None
            try:
                return super().execute(user_id, top_n_categories=top_n_categories)
            finally:
                self.as_of_timestamp = saved

    cases = {c.case_id: c for c in load_cases(builder.DATASET_PATH)}
    case = cases["correctness_031"]
    ev = _evaluator(world, tool_factory=NoExecuteFilter)
    metrics = ev.score_component(ev.run_component(case.input), case.expected)
    assert metrics["all_fields_match"] == 0.0
    assert metrics["n_interactions_correct"] == 0.0
    assert metrics["future_leakage"] == 1.0
```

```python
def test_oracle_and_realistic_are_separate_groups_and_no_overall_group():
    ra1, rb1 = _runs(2, 25, "similar", lambda i, r: 0.0, lambda i, r: 1.0)
    ra2, rb2 = _runs(2, 25, "constrained", lambda i, r: 1.0, lambda i, r: 0.0)
    ra = [x + y for x, y in zip(ra1, ra2)]
    rb = [x + y for x, y in zip(rb1, rb2)]
    res = analyze_metric(ra, rb, A, B, "task_success")
    assert res["realistic"]["diff"] == 1.0 and res["oracle"]["diff"] == -1.0
    assert not any(k in res for k in ("all", "overall", "total"))
    assert any("ORACLE" in w for w in res["oracle"]["warnings"])
```

```python
def test_unscored_baseline_is_counted_not_dropped():
    """personalized: planner w/o MemoryTool has task_success=None -> no pairs, reported explicitly."""
    ra, rb = _runs(3, 30, "personalized", lambda i, r: None, lambda i, r: 1.0)
    g = analyze_metric(ra, rb, A, B, "task_success")["realistic/personalized"]
    assert g["verdict"] == "no_pairs" and g["n_unscored_baseline"] == 30 and g["n_paired"] == 0
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_cli_end_to_end(tmp_path, capsys, monkeypatch):
    dirs = []
    for run in range(3):
        d = tmp_path / f"exp_{run}"
        d.mkdir()
        rows = (_rows(A, "similar", "warm", 25, lambda i, r: float(i % 2 == 0), run)
                + _rows(B, "similar", "warm", 25, lambda i, r: 1.0, run)
                + _rows(B, "constrained", "warm", 25, lambda i, r: 1.0, run))
        (d / "scored.jsonl").write_text("\n".join(json.dumps(r) for r in rows), encoding="utf-8")
        dirs.append(str(d))
    out = tmp_path / "out"
    # resamples/dirs/output dir đi qua CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH (configs/eval/memory_utility.yaml + data_paths.outputs)
    overlay = tmp_path / "overlay.yaml"
    overlay.write_text(yaml.safe_dump({
        "eval/memory_utility": {"memory_utility": {"resamples": 500, "dirs": dirs}},
        "path/data_paths": {"outputs": {"memory_utility": str(out)}},
    }), encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.setenv("CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH", str(overlay))
    assert main([]) == 0
    data = json.loads((out / "memory_utility.json").read_text())
    assert data["meta"]["runs_a"] == 3
    assert data["result"]["task_success"]["realistic/similar"]["verdict"] == "memory_better"
    assert data["result"]["task_success"]["oracle/constrained"]["verdict"] == "no_pairs"
    assert (out / "memory_utility.md").exists()
```

```python
def test_compare_metrics():

    baseline_metrics = {

        "error_rate_mean": 0.1,
        "latency_mean": 0.5,
    }
    current_metrics = {

        "error_rate_mean": 0.05,

    assert changes["success_rate"]["current"] == 0.95
    assert changes["success_rate"]["delta"] == pytest.approx(0.05)

    assert changes["error_rate"]["significant"] is True
```

```python
def test_compare_benchmarks_full():
    """Test full benchmark comparison."""
    baseline = BenchmarkSummary(
        component="memory",
        metrics={"success_rate_mean": 0.9, "error_rate_mean": 0.1},
        latency={"mean": 0.5, "p95": 1.0},
        tags_summary={"normal": {"success_rate_mean": 0.9}},
    )
    current = BenchmarkSummary(
        component="memory",
        metrics={"success_rate_mean": 0.95, "error_rate_mean": 0.05},
        latency={"mean": 0.6, "p95": 1.2},
        tags_summary={"normal": {"success_rate_mean": 0.95}},
    )

    report = compare_benchmarks(baseline, current, metric_threshold=0.05, latency_threshold=0.1)

    assert report.baseline_summary == baseline
    assert report.current_summary == current
    assert "success_rate" in report.metric_changes
    assert "mean" in report.latency_changes
    assert "normal" in report.tag_changes
    assert report.summary in ["improved", "regressed", "neutral"]
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/component/test_regression.py::test_compare_benchmarks_roundtrip`  (0.0019s)

_Test that RegressionReport can be serialized and deserialized._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_compare_benchmarks_roundtrip():
    """Test that RegressionReport can be serialized and deserialized."""
    import json

    baseline = BenchmarkSummary(
        component="memory",
        metrics={"success_rate_mean": 0.9},
        latency={"mean": 0.5},
    )
    current = BenchmarkSummary(
        component="memory",
        metrics={"success_rate_mean": 0.95},
        latency={"mean": 0.6},
    )

    report = compare_benchmarks(baseline, current)

    # Serialize
    report_dict = report.to_dict()
    report_json = json.dumps(report_dict)

    # Deserialize
    report_dict_loaded = json.loads(report_json)
    report_loaded = type(report).from_dict(report_dict_loaded)

    assert report_loaded.summary == report.summary
    assert report_loaded.baseline_summary.component == report.baseline_summary.component
    assert report_loaded.current_summary.component == report.current_summary.component
```

```python
def test_aggregate_maps_gate_labels(tmp_path):
```
